Let's design an iterative method based on "flattening" with active sets, using FFT, for n maybe 1024/2048. We can start from exponential (λ=1.2) or random. We will iterate:
- p positive sum1.
- c=conv(p,p) (FFT).
- M=max c.
- Choose active weights w_k = (c_k - (1-δ)M)_+ or softmax.
- Compute gradient g_j = ∑ w_k p_{k-j} (ordered? derivative factor 2; ignore constant). Need correct orientation.
- Update p_j ← p_j * exp(-η g_j / scale). Normalize.
This is multiplicative descent for the weighted sum of c_k. It can reduce active high coefficients. We can adapt η and restart.

Will this escape uniform? Start from non-symmetric exponential, not stuck. It may converge to a local minimum with score maybe <1.5. We can use multiple initial shapes: exponential increasing/decreasing, sine-modified uniform, two-block, random FFT noise, etc.

We can also include a "random perturbation" phase: if stuck, add noise and re-run.

Now, final code complexity. Need ensure no closures/lambdas. We can define top-level functions:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys

def _normalize_nonneg(vec):
   x=np.array(vec,float); x=np.where(x>0, x,0); s=x.sum(); if s<=0: return None; return x/s

def _fft_len(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _fft_conv_real(a,b):
   n=len(a)+len(b)-1; L=1 << (n-1).bit_length(); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:n]; return c

def _score_array(p): mimics evaluate but normalized? Use if sum<0.01; clip? For search p positive.
   n=p.size; c=_fft_conv_real(p,p); M=np.max(c); S=p.sum(); return 2*n*M/(S*S), M, c

def _update(p, c, beta=10.0, eta=0.5, threshold=None):
   M=np.max(c); w=np.exp(beta*(c-M)); w/=w.sum(); w_rev=w[::-1]; g2=_fft_conv_real(p,w_rev); # length 3n-2
   L=2*n-1; idx = L-1 - np.arange(n); g = g2[idx[:n]]; g=np.maximum(g,0);
   g = g / (np.mean(g)+1e-300); # or g/(2*n?)
   p2 = p * np.exp(-eta*g)
   p2=np.maximum(p2, 1e-300); p2/=p2.sum(); return p2, g
```

```
   for eta in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,0.1,0.05]:
```

Update details:
```
def step_update(p, c, eta, beta_factor):
    L=2*n-1; M=c.max()
    # weights using log-sum
    if M>0:
       beta=beta_factor/M
       z=beta*(c-M)
       w=np.exp(z - z.max()); w/=w.sum()
    else uniform
    w_rev=w[::-1]
    conv_len = len(p)+len(w_rev)-1 = 3n-2
    full = fft_conv(p, w_rev)
    idx = np.arange(n-1, L-1, -1)? Need idx = L-1-j => j=0->L-1, j=n-1->L-n. So idx = np.arange(L-1, L-n, -1). length n.
    g=full[idx]
    g=np.maximum(g,0)
    gmean=g.mean()+tiny
    g=g/gmean
    arg=eta*(1-g)  # p *= exp(arg)
    arg=np.clip(arg, -4,4)
    p2=p*np.exp(arg)
    p2=np.maximum(p2, tiny)
    p2/=p2.sum()
    return p2
```

Let's outline code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_FT_EPS = 1e-300

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _fft_conv(a,b):
    n = len(a)+len(b)-1
    L = _next_pow2(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return c[:n]

def _normalize(arr):
    x=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x)&(x>0), x, 0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 0: x=np.ones_like(x); s=x.sum()
    return x/s

def _fft_score(p):
    n=p.size
    c=_fft_conv(p,p)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    S=float(p.sum())
    if S <=0 or not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf, c
    return float(2*n*M/(S*S)), c

def _exact_score(p):
    # mirror evaluate_sequence but for array
    x=np.asarray(p,float)
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <0.01: return np.inf
    c=np.convolve(x,x)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2*x.size*M/(s*s))
```

For p sum1, exact score direct.

Update:
```
def _update_p(p, c, eta=0.5, beta=15.0, tiny=1e-300):
    n=p.size
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M <= tiny:
       return p
    z = beta*(c-M)
    z -= z.max()
    w=np.exp(z)
    wsum=w.sum()
    if wsum <= tiny: w=np.ones_like(c)/c.size
    else: w/=wsum
    w_rev=w[::-1]
    full=_fft_conv(p,w_rev)
    L=2*n-1
    idx = np.arange(L-1, L-n, -1)  # length n? L-1 down to L-n inclusive
    g=full[idx]
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    gmean=float(g.mean())
    if gmean <= tiny: gmean=1.0
    g = g/gmean
    arg = eta*(1.0 - g)
    np.clip(arg, -3.0, 3.0, out=arg)
    p2 = p * np.exp(arg)
    p2 = np.where(np.isfinite(p2) & (p2>0), p2, tiny)
    p2 /= p2.sum()
    return p2
```
Check `idx` length: `np.arange(stop, start, step)` inclusive? In numpy, arange(start, stop, step) stops before stop. Need include L-n. Use `np.arange(L-1, L-n-1, -1)`. Since L-n = n-1. For n=4,L=7, idx should 6,5,4,3. np.arange(6,7-4-1=2,-1) ->6,5,4,3 good.

Beta: We pass beta as factor times 1/M? In `_update_p`, z=beta*(c-M). beta is factor (dimensionless); if factor 10, z max0, typical c-M maybe (0.25M?) z=-2.5. Good. Use factor maybe 5-20.

Define `run_smoothing(p, budget, rng, ...)`:
```
def _smooth_initial(p, budget_seconds, rng, current_best_score):
    p=np.array(p,float); p=_normalize(p)
    score,c=_fft_score(p)
    best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=score
    eta=0.3
    beta_factor=8.0
    stagnant=0
    it=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < start + budget_seconds:
       # optionally update beta_factor each iter
       beta_factor = min(20.0, 6.0 + 0.005*(it % 1000))? 
       improved=False
       for candidate_eta in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta, 0.05):
           p2=_update_p(p,c,candidate_eta,beta_factor)
           s2,c2=_fft_score(p2)
           if s2 < score * (1-1e-10) and np.isfinite(s2):
              p=p2; score=s2; c=c2; improved=True
              if s2 < best_local_score:
                 best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=s2
              break
       if improved:
          stagnant=0
          eta=min(1.5, eta*1.05)
       else:
          stagnant+=1
          eta=max(0.05, eta*0.7)
          if stagnant > 30:
             # perturb
             p = _normalize(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,p.size)))
             score,c=_fft_score(p)
             stagnant=0
          elif stagnant > 10:
             p = _normalize(p + 0.01*rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p.size)))
             score,c=_fft_score(p)
       it+=1
       # exact check every 200 maybe
    return best_local, best_local_score
```

```
def _make_starts(n, rng, seed_map=None):
```

For block starts, ensure length not zero:
```
def _block_start(n, m, second_len, gap, alpha):
  p=np.zeros(n)
  m=int(m); gap=int(gap); l=int(second_len)
  if m<=0 or l<=0: return None
  end=m+gap+l
  if end>n: l=n-(m+gap); if l<=0 return None
  p[:m]=alpha/m
  if l>0: p[m+gap:m+gap+l]=(1-alpha)/l
  p=np.maximum(p,0)
  s=p.sum()
  if s<=0: return None
  return p/s
```
Then in make starts, loop params.

Search function `_smooth_start(p, time_limit, rng, beta_start=8)`:

```
def _smooth_start(p, time_limit, rng, dead_at=None):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p=_normalize(p)
   score,c=_fft_score(p)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=score
   eta=0.25
   stagnant=0
   it=0
   start=time.monotonic()
   if time_limit <=0: return best,best_score
   t_end=start+max(0.1,time_limit)
   while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       # beta factor schedule
       beta_factor = 8.0 + 4.0*(it % 200)/200.0  # 8-12? maybe
       improved=False
       # try current and smaller
       for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
           et=eta*factor
           if et <= 0.01: et=0.01
           p2=_update_p(p,c,et,beta_factor)
           s2,c2=_fft_score(p2)
           if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < score*(1-1e-10):
              p=p2; score=s2; c=c2; improved=True
              if s2 < best_score:
                  best=p.copy(); best_score=s2
              break
       if improved:
          stagnant=0
          eta=min(1.0, eta*1.07)
       else:
          stagnant += 1
          eta=max(0.03, eta*0.8)
          if stagnant > 50:
             # larger perturbation
             p = _normalize(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,p.size)))
             score,c=_fft_score(p)
             stagnant=0
             # if perturbation worse than best, maybe revert? Could keep p local. Better if perturb too harmful, revert to best fraction.
             if not np.isfinite(score) or score > best_score*1.5:
                 p = _normalize(best + 0.05*rng.dirichlet(np.ones(best.size)))
                 score,c=_fft_score(p)
          elif stagnant > 20:
             p = _normalize(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,p.size)))
             score,c=_fft_score(p)
       it +=1
   return best,best_score
```
Potential problem: If best is very good but current p worsens, we might lose local track. Use `p = _normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,p.size)))`. This keeps near best. Good.

Define top-level `_fourier_p(theta, n, K)`:
```
def _fourier_p(theta,n,K):
   idx=np.arange(n)
   f=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      ang=2*math.pi*k*idx/n
      f += theta[2*k-2]*np.cos(ang) + theta[2*k-1]*np.sin(ang)
   f -= np.max(f)
   p=np.exp(np.clip(f,-50,50))
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: p=np.ones(n); s=n
   return p/s
```
This loops K every call; for gradient also need cos/sin arrays. For efficiency, define `_fourier_basis(n,K)` returns list of cos/sin? Could recompute. K small.

`_fourier_basis(n,K)` top-level? It returns tuple of arrays length n for each mode? Memory K*n*2. For n=1024,K64 => 131k, fine. But simple loop okay.

Objective and gradient compute c via `_fft_conv`; gradient needs correlation. We can reuse `_correlation_p_w(p,w,n)` helper:
```
def _correlation_p_w(p,w,n):
   L=2*n-1; w_rev=w[::-1]; full=_fft_conv(p,w_rev); idx=np.arange(L-1,L-n-1,-1); g=full[idx]; g=np.maximum(g,0); return g
```
This returns ∑ w_k p_{k-j}. Derivative factor 2 in objective. Use in update and gradient.

Fourier objective:
```
def _fourier_obj(theta,n,K,beta_factor):
   p=_fourier_p(theta,n,K); c=_fft_conv(p,p); M=np.max(c); beta=beta_factor/n
   z=beta*(c-M); w=np.exp(z-np.max(z)); s=w.sum(); if s==0: return 0
   return M + np.log(s)/beta
```
Since logsumexp = M + log s (with z=β(c-M); max z=0, s=multiplicity of max approx). This returns smoothed M. Good.

Gradient:
```
def _fourier_grad(theta,n,K,beta_factor):
   p=_fourier_p; c; M; beta; z; w; w/=w.sum()
   A=2*_correlation_p_w(p,w,n)
   pA=np.dot(p,A)
   Af=p*(A-pA)
   grad=np.zeros_like(theta)
   idx=np.arange(n)
   for k in range(1,K+1): ang=2π k idx/n; grad[2k-2]=np.dot(Af, np.cos(ang)); grad[2k-1]=np.dot(Af,np.sin(ang))
   return grad
```
This should be correct up to derivative of f max? softmax derivative invariant to max, okay.

Now, L-BFGS on nonconvex may need good beta. Use beta_factor=1000? If objective M + (1/β)log m, with β=3000/n, M~0.001, 1/β~0.0003, max multiplicity factor small. Good. For n512, 1/β=0.0006. Use 2000/n maybe.

Detailed implementations:

`_fft_conv`:
```
def _fft_conv(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0 or b.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
    out_len=a.size+b.size-1
    L=1 << max(0, (out_len-1).bit_length())
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return c[:out_len]
```
For out_len=1, (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. good.

`_fft_score`:
```
def _fft_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf, None
    c=_fft_conv(p,p)
    M=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    S=float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or S <=0: return np.inf, c
    return 2*n*M/(S*S), c
```
No clipping; search p nonneg.

`_exact_score` mirrors evaluator with clipping:
```
x=np.asarray(p,float); x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, np.nan); x=np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x>=0), x, 0.0); x=np.minimum(x,1000); s=x.sum(); if s<0.01 return inf; c=np.convolve(x,x); M=float(np.max(c)); return 2*x.size*M/(s*s)
```

`_add_candidate`:
```
def _add_candidate(candidates, limit, score, p):
   if p is None: return
   try:
     arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
   except: return
   if arr.size==0: return
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr) & (arr>0), arr, 0.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s <=0: return
   if not np.isfinite(score): return
   candidates.append((float(score), arr.copy()))
   if len(candidates) > limit:
      candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
      candidates.pop()
```
Note evaluates score from search maybe unnormalized; p normalized. If score not finite skip.

`_correlation_p_w`:
```
def _correlation_p_w(p,w):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); w=np.asarray(w,float); n=p.size
   L=2*n-1
   if L != w.size:
      if w.size < L: pad; else trim. But w from c length L. 
   w_rev=w[::-1]
   full=_fft_conv(p,w_rev)
   idx=np.arange(L-1, L-n-1, -1)
   g=full[idx]
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
   return g
```
If n=1, L=1, idx arange(0,-1,-1) [0].

```
def _finalize(candidates, best_p, best_score, deadline):
   arr_best = best_p if best_p is not None else np.array([1/3,2/3])
   final_p=arr_best; final_score=np.inf
   # evaluate exact for candidates top and final
   eval_ps=[final_p]

       if p.size <= 5000: eval_ps.append(p)
   for p in eval_ps:
       seq=np.asarray(p,float); seq=np.clip(seq,0,1000); ssum=seq.sum(); if ssum<0.01: seq*=0.01/sum? ensure
       sc=_exact_score(seq)
       if sc<final_score: final_p=seq; final_score=sc
   # if final_score inf use fallback
   try evaluate_sequence
```

One subtlety: In `_fourier_obj`, `M = float(np.max(c))`; if c has tiny negative but M positive. `z = beta*(c-M)` can be large negative; use `z -= z.max()` (0). `s = np.sum(np.exp(z))`. If s=0 due underflow (all very negative impossible since max gives 1), s≥1. Return `M + math.log(s)/beta`? Use `np.log(s)`. Since β maybe float.

In gradient, A=2*corr. `pA = float(np.dot(p,A))`. `Af=p*(A-pA)`. Then dot with cos/sin. The derivative sign? If p=softmax(f), L(f) depends on p; dL/dθ = ∑_j (∂L/∂p_j) dp_j/dθ_j. ∂L/∂p_j = A_j. dp_j/dθ_a = p_j (∂f_j/∂θ_a - ∑_i p_i ∂f_i/∂θ_a). Thus dot A_j p_j ... = Af dot df. Correct.

If `K=0`? not.

Now, `_try_fourier`:
```

   try: from scipy.optimize import minimize

   time limit check

```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative output sequence minimizing

    score(p) = 2 * len(p) * max(convolve(p, p)) / sum(p)^2

The program combines several bounded search strategies:
  * small-length global/composed Nelder-Mead / differential evolution,
  * low-dimensional Fourier-parameter L-BFGS optimization,
  * FFT-based multiplicative log-sum flattening local search.

It always returns a finite non-negative list, and keeps the best exact score
seen during the time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = a.size + b.size - 1
    L = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, L)
    return c[:out_len]


def _normalize(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x > 0.0), x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        x = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.sum())
    return x / s


def _fft_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0)
    c = _fft_conv(p, p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    S = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or S <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), c
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S)), c


def _exact_score(p):
    x = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x >= 0.0), x, 0.0)
    x = np.minimum(x, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if x.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(x, x)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * x.size * M / (s * s))


def _add_candidate(candidates, limit, score, p):
    if p is None:
        return
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr) & (arr >= 0.0), arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(score) or s <= 0.0:
        return
    candidates.append((float(score), arr.copy()))
    if len(candidates) > limit:
        candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
        candidates.pop()


def _correlation_p_w(p, w):
    """Return g[j] = sum_k w[k] * p[k-j] for j = 0..len(p)-1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(w.size, L)
        tmp[:m] = w[:m]
        w = tmp
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    full = _fft_conv(p, w_rev)
    idx = np.arange(L - 1, L - n - 1, -1)
    if idx.size != n:
        idx = np.arange(n - 1, L - n - 1, -1)
        if idx.size != n:
            idx = np.arange(n)[::-1]
    g = full[idx]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
    return g


def _update_p(p, c, eta, sharp):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return p

    z = sharp * (c - M) / max(M, 1e-300)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones_like(w, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, w.size)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    g = _correlation_p_w(p, w)
    gm = float(g.mean())
    if gm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(gm):
        gm = 1.0
    g = g / gm

    arg = eta * (1.0 - g)
    arg = np.clip(arg, -3.5, 3.5)
    p2 = p * np.exp(arg)
    p2 = np.where(np.isfinite(p2) & (p2 > 0.0), p2, 1e-300)
    p2 = p2 / max(1e-300, float(p2.sum()))
    return p2


def _smooth_start(p, time_limit, rng):
    p = _normalize(p)
    score, c = _fft_score(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = score

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = score
        return best, best_score

    eta = 0.25
    sharp = 8.0
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    end_t = start_t + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() >= end_t:
            break
        if it > 0 and it % 100 == 0:
            sharp = min(22.0, sharp + 0.75)

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            et = float(eta) * mult
            if et <= 0.005:
                et = 0.005
            p2 = _update_p(p, c, et, sharp)
            s2, c2 = _fft_score(p2)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < score - 1e-13:
                p = p2
                score = s2
                c = c2
                improved = True
                if s2 < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = s2
                break

        if improved:
            stagnant = 0
            eta = min(1.5, eta * 1.05)
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.78)

            if stagnant >= 60:
                # Restart near current best with larger multiplicative noise.
                noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=p.size))
                p = _normalize(best * noise)
                score, c = _fft_score(p)
                stagnant = 0
                if not np.isfinite(score) or score > best_score * 1.6:
                    p = _normalize(best + 0.03 * rng.dirichlet(np.ones(best.size)))
                    score, c = _fft_score(p)
            elif stagnant >= 25:
                noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=p.size))
                p = _normalize(p * noise)
                score, c = _fft_score(p)
                if not np.isfinite(score) or score > best_score * 1.25:
                    p = _normalize(best + 0.01 * rng.dirichlet(np.ones(best.size)))
                    score, c = _fft_score(p)

        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def _block_start(n, m, gap, l, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)
    gap = int(gap)
    l = int(l)
    if m <= 0 or l <= 0 or gap < 0:
        return None
    end = m + gap + l
    if end > n:
        return None
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:m] = float(alpha) / float(m)
    p[m + gap:m + gap + l] = float(1.0 - alpha) / float(l)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p) & (p > 0.0), p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return p / s


def _smooth_random_start(n, rng):
    f = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
    k = max(3, n // 25)
    ker = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    f = np.convolve(f, ker, mode="same")
    p = np.exp(0.35 * f)
    return _normalize(p)


def _average_downsample(x, n_out):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x >= 0.0), x, 0.0)
    n_out = int(n_out)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n_out, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n_out))
    if x.size == n_out:
        return _normalize(x)
    if x.size > n_out:
        edges = np.linspace(0, x.size, n_out + 1).astype(int)
        out = np.zeros(n_out, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n_out):
            seg = x[edges[i]:edges[i + 1]]
            if seg.size:
                out[i] = float(np.mean(seg))
        return _normalize(out)
    # Upsample by interpolation.
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_out)
    out = np.interp(xn, xo, x)
    return _normalize(out)


def _make_starts(n, rng):
    starts = []
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return starts
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    x01 = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))

    # One-sided exponential shapes.
    for lam in (0.4, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8, 2.6, 3.6):
        p = np.exp(-lam * x01)
        starts.append(_normalize(p))
        starts.append(_normalize(p[::-1]))

    # Power-law shapes.
    for q in (0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.6, 2.5):
        p = (1.0 - x01) ** q
        starts.append(_normalize(p + 1e-9))
        p = (1.0 - x[::-1]) ** q
        starts.append(_normalize(p + 1e-9))

    # Sin/cos perturbations of the flat shape.
    for amp in (0.25, 0.45, 0.65, 0.85):
        for ph in (0.0, math.pi / 4.0, math.pi / 2.0, 3.0 * math.pi / 4.0):
            p = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x01 + ph)
            p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
            starts.append(_normalize(p))
            p = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x01 + ph)
            p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
            starts.append(_normalize(p))

    # Block/gap shapes.
    for mf in (0.25, 0.33, 0.42, 0.5, 0.62, 0.75):
        m = max(1, int(round(n * mf)))
        for gf in (0.0, 0.08, 0.18, 0.30):
            gap = int(round(n * gf))
            remaining = n - m - gap
            lf_remain = int(round(remaining * 0.55))
            if lf_remain < 1 or m + gap + lf_remain > n:
                continue
            for alpha in (0.25, 0.45, 0.65, 0.80):
                b = _block_start(n, m, gap, lf_remain, alpha)
                if b is not None:
                    starts.append(b)
                b = _block_start(n, n - m, gap, m, alpha)
                if b is not None:
                    starts.append(b)

    # Random smooth starts.
    for _ in range(4):
        starts.append(_smooth_random_start(n, rng))

    # Remove exact duplicates is not necessary; cap the list for time control.
    if len(starts) > 28:
        starts = starts[:28]
    return starts


def _theta_to_p(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(theta.size) + 1
    w = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        w[1:] = np.exp(np.clip(theta, -25.0, 25.0))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return w / s


def _theta_score(theta):
    p = _theta_to_p(theta)
    return _exact_score(p)


def _try_small_de(rng, candidates, limit, deadline):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    except Exception:
        return
    for n in (3, 4, 5, 6, 7):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
            break
        try:
            bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0)] * max(1, n - 1)
            seed = int(rng.integers(0, 1_000_000_000))
            res = differential_evolution(
                _theta_score,
                bounds,
                seed=seed,
                maxiter=45,
                popsize=14,
                tol=1e-7,
                polish=True,
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                p = _theta_to_p(res.x)
                s = _exact_score(p)
                _add_candidate(candidates, limit, s, p)
        except Exception:
            pass


def _fourier_p(theta, n, K):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    two_pi = 2.0 * math.pi
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = two_pi * float(k) * idx / float(n)
        a = float(theta[2 * k - 2]) if 2 * k - 2 < theta.size else 0.0
        b = float(theta[2 * k - 1]) if 2 * k - 1 < theta.size else 0.0
        f += a * np.cos(ang) + b * np.sin(ang)
    f = f - float(np.max(f))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(f, -40.0, 40.0))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.sum())
    return p / s


def _fourier_obj(theta, n, K, beta_factor):
    p = _fourier_p(theta, n, K)
    c = _fft_conv(p, p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return 1e100
    beta = max(1e-12, float(beta_factor) / float(n))
    z = beta * (c - M)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    ss = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return M
    return M + float(np.log(ss)) / beta


def _fourier_grad(theta, n, K, beta_factor):
    p = _fourier_p(theta, n, K)
    c = _fft_conv(p, p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.zeros_like(theta, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = max(1e-12, float(beta_factor) / float(n))
    z = beta * (c - M)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones_like(w, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, w.size)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    A = 2.0 * _correlation_p_w(p, w)
    pA = float(np.dot(p, A))
    Af = p * (A - pA)

    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    grad = np.zeros(theta.size, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    two_pi = 2.0 * math.pi
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = two_pi * float(k) * idx / float(n)
        co = np.cos(ang)
        si = np.sin(ang)
        gi = 2 * k - 2
        if gi < grad.size:
            grad[gi] = float(np.dot(Af, co))
        gj = 2 * k - 1
        if gj < grad.size:
            grad[gj] = float(np.dot(Af, si))
    return grad


def _try_fourier(rng, candidates, limit, deadline):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import minimize
    except Exception:
        return

    plans = (
        (512, 24, 2600.0, 140),
        (1024, 32, 3200.0, 170),
        (1536, 40, 3600.0, 130),
    )

    for n, K, beta_factor, maxiter in plans:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240.0:
            break
        starts = []
        starts.append(np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64))
        starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=2 * K))
        a = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
        a[0] = -0.35
        a[1] = 0.55
        a[3] = 0.20
        starts.append(a)
        a = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
        a[0] = 0.25
        a[1] = -0.45
        a[2] = 0.10
        starts.append(a)

        for th in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            try:
                x0 = np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64)
                res = minimize(
                    _fourier_obj,
                    x0,
                    args=(n, K, beta_factor),
                    jac=_fourier_grad,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
                )
                for x in (x0, getattr(res, "x", None)):
                    if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))):
                        continue
                    p = _fourier_p(x, n, K)
                    s, _ = _fft_score(p)
                    _add_candidate(candidates, limit, s, p)
            except Exception:
                pass


def _finalize(candidates, best_p, best_score, deadline):
    fallback = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    final_p = best_p if best_p is not None else fallback
    final_score = best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")

    eval_ps = [final_p]
    seen = set()
    for _, p in candidates[:16]:
        if p is None:
            continue
        key = (p.size, float(np.sum(p)))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        eval_ps.append(p)
        if len(eval_ps) >= 24:
            break

    for p in eval_ps:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr) & (arr >= 0.0), arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        if s < 0.01:
            arr = arr * (0.1 / max(1e-300, s))
        arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
        sc = _exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = sc
            final_p = arr

    # Authoritative exact evaluator, if present.
    try:
        seq = [float(v) for v in final_p]
        sc2 = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < final_score:
            final_score = sc2
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_p is None or final_p.size == 0:
        final_p = fallback
        final_score = _exact_score(fallback_p)

    # Last safety: force valid finite non-negative values.
    out = np.asarray(final_p, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out) & (out >= 0.0), out, 0.0)
    out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if out.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    # The evaluator accepts sum >= 0.01; this prevents pathological tiny outputs.
    if s < 0.01:
        out = out * (0.05 / s)
    return [float(v) for v in out], float(final_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point; returns the best sequence found within budget."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    budget_s = max(10.0, budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 45.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 12345)
    candidates = []
    limit = 48

    # Guarantee at least one valid candidate.
    fallback = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    fallback_score = _exact_score(fallback)
    _add_candidate(candidates, limit, fallback_score, fallback)

    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    if candidates:
        best_score = float(candidates[0][0])
        best_p = candidates[0][1].copy()

    print("initial best_score = %.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # 1. Small-length global search: sometimes finite-length effects are valuable.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        print("phase: small DE search", flush=True)
        _try_small_de(rng, candidates, limit, deadline - 120.0)
        if candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
            if candidates[0][1].size and np.isfinite(candidates[0][0]):
                best_score = float(candidates[0][0])
                best_p = candidates[0][1].copy()
        print("after small DE: best_score = %.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # 2. Smooth low-coordinate Fourier optimization for medium-large lengths.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        print("phase: Fourier L-BFGS", flush=True)
        _try_fourier(rng, candidates, limit, deadline - 300.0)
        if candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
            if candidates[0][1].size and np.isfinite(candidates[0][0]):
                best_score = float(candidates[0][0])
                best_p = candidates[0][1].copy()
        print("after Fourier: best_score = %.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # 3. Big flexible search at a chosen length. Starting from a moderate length
    #    gives good continuous relaxation; later we can upsample the best result.
    N_MAIN = 1024
    starts = _make_starts(N_MAIN, rng)

    # Include good candidate shapes of the same length, if any.
    for _, p in candidates:
        if p is not None and p.size == N_MAIN:
            starts.append(_normalize(p))
    # Include parent if available, downsampled to N_MAIN.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.size > 0:
            starts.append(_average_downsample(parent, N_MAIN))
    except Exception:
        pass
    # Include upsampled current best as a strong start.
    if best_p is not None and best_p.size != N_MAIN:
        starts.append(_average_downsample(best_p, N_MAIN))

    if not starts:
        starts = [np.ones(N_MAIN, dtype=np.float64) / float(N_MAIN)]

    if len(starts) > 18:
        starts = starts[:18]

    # 3a. Quick pass on all starts to see which basins look promising.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        print("phase: quick FFT local search, n=%d" % N_MAIN, flush=True)
        used = min(len(starts), 18)
        for i in range(used):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
                break
            quick_t = min(12.0, max(2.0, (deadline - 180.0 - time.monotonic()) / max(1, used - i)))
            p, s = _smooth_start(starts[i], quick_t, rng)
            _add_candidate(candidates, limit, s, p)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best_score = float(s)
                best_p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
        print("after quick pass: best_score = %.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # 3b. Deeper refinement of top basins.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        print("phase: deep FFT local search, top basins, n=%d" % N_MAIN, flush=True)
        top = []
        seen_sizes = set()
        for _, p in candidates:
            if p is None:
                continue
            if p.size != N_MAIN:
                continue
            if numpy_arr_key := (p.size, round(float(p.sum()), 12), round(float(p[0]), 12), round(float(p[-1]), 12)):
                if numpy_arr_key not in seen_sizes:
                    seen_sizes.add(numpy_arr_key)
                    top.append(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))
            if len(top) >= 4:
                break
        if not top:
            top = [np.asarray(starts[0], dtype=np.float64)]

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        each = max(25.0, (remaining - 60.0) / float(len(top)))
        for p in top:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
                break
            p2, s2 = _smooth_start(p, each, rng)
            _add_candidate(candidates, limit, s2, p2)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = float(s2)
                best_p = np.asarray(p2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
        print("after deep pass: best_score = %.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # 4. Optional larger-length refinement from the best medium-length shape.
    N_LARGE = 2048
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0:
        print("phase: larger refinement, n=%d" % N_LARGE, flush=True)
        if best_p is not None:
            large_starts = [_average_downsample(best_p, N_LARGE)]
        else:
            large_starts = [np.ones(N_LARGE, dtype=np.float64) / float(N_LARGE)]
        large_starts.append(_smooth_random_start(N_LARGE, rng))
        large_starts.append(_normalize(np.exp(-1.5 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N_LARGE, dtype=np.float64))))

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 45.0
        each = max(20.0, remaining / max(1, len(large_starts)))
        for p in large_starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40.0:
                break
            p2, s2 = _smooth_start(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), each, rng)
            _add_candidate(candidates, limit, s2, p2)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = float(s2)
                best_p = np.asarray(p2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
        print("after larger refinement: best_score = %.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # Final exact evaluation of the best candidates.
    out_seq, final_score = _finalize(candidates, best_p, best_score, deadline)
    print("final_score = %.9f, length = %d" % (final_score, len(out_seq)), flush=True)
    return out_seq
```
